Researchers propose the Skip RNN model to address challenges in training Recurrent Neural Networks on long sequences, such as slow inference and difficulty in capturing long-term dependencies, by learning to skip state updates and reduce the computational graph size.

535
This research paper proposes a novel meta-learning framework called Transductive Propagation Network (TPN) to address the low-data problem in few-shot learning. TPN classifies the entire test set at once by propagating labels from labeled instances to unlabeled test instances using a learned graph structure.

A novel approach, Attentive Weights Generation for few shot learning via Information Maximization (AWGIM), is proposed to address limitations in generating universal classification weights for diverse query samples from limited training data.

This paper introduces Sliced-Wasserstein Autoencoders (SWAE), a generative model that enables shaping the latent space distribution by regularizing the autoencoder loss with the sliced-Wasserstein distance of encoded samples to a predefined samplable distribution.

This research paper proposes the Information Maximising Autoencoder (InfoAE), an unsupervised model that learns powerful disentangled representations by maximising mutual information between the representation and given information, achieving 98.9% accuracy on the MNIST dataset.

This research paper introduces OvA-INN, a novel method for Continual Learning that learns one class at a time without storing previous data, improving accuracy by stacking invertible networks on top of feature extractors and outperforming state-of-the-art approaches on MNIST and CIFAR-100 datasets, achieving 72% accuracy on CIFAR-100.

2439
Researchers developed Merged-Averaged Classifiers via Hashing (MACH), a novel $K$-classification algorithm for large $K$. It reduces memory usage to $O(d\log{K})$ and inference cost to $O(K\log{K} + d\log{K})$.

1164
This paper presents a non-asymptotic analysis of the Stochastic Variance Reduced Gradient (SVRG) algorithm under a noisy least squares regression problem. It compares SVRG to Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD), showing a trade-off between computational cost and convergence speed in underparameterized neural networks, with SVRG outperforming SGD after a few epochs.

Researchers propose two methods, Trace-norm regression (TNR) and Stable Trace-norm Analysis (StaTNA), to enhance recommender systems using side information, outperforming state-of-the-art models on the MovieLens dataset.

2996
A new autoencoding model is proposed, combining the strengths of variational autoencoders (VAE) and generative adversarial networks (GAN). It optimizes λ-Jeffreys divergence and balances mode-seeking and mode-covering behavior, achieving state-of-the-art results on CIFAR-10 and TinyImagent datasets.

2290
This research paper bridges the gap between Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and Variational AutoEncoders (VAEs) by showing that an optimal transport GAN with entropy regularization can be viewed as a generative model maximizing a lower-bound on average sample likelihoods.
